HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000299/B002","root_000299/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-7","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_0bccbefee0483cb4e255","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:11:4\",\"root_000299\",[\"B002\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Yeni kalma ve insanlar arasında anlatılan hikayeye dönüşme boyutları, eski kurtarılışların daha sonraki anlatımla canlı ve örnek bir ortak hafıza halinde tutulmasını sağlar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000299/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_40f0895c6016b55022c6","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:11:4\",\"root_000299\",[\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Anlatmanın kalbi öğütle parlatma boyutu, geçmiş bakımı hatırlatmayı terk edilme kuşkusuna ve davranıştaki katılığa karşı bir iç düzeltmeye dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_54d627e9a9a04000dfe5","text":"No later ayah exists in this clipped window, so no later surprise emerged. Retrospective rereading confirmed that 93:6–10 supplies the concrete favor-history and ethical correction on which the fixed reporting command operates.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000299/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5db1f803011044115856","text":"2. **Reporting discloses and renews what happened.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ح د ث:B006` is disclosure or bringing out, and fixed `ح د ث:B001` is something coming into being after not being. **Structural cue:** the preceding sequence brings an isolated, unlocated, and needy condition into shelter, direction, and sufficiency; the final imperative then brings that sequence into speech. **Assigned role:** disclosure makes hidden care visible; coming-into-being models its new presence in shared memory. **Abductive move:** I infer narration → renewed social presence of the event; speech does not recreate the original acts materially. **Reading change:** the command is performative in a limited sense: telling makes previously private favor newly operative as public knowledge.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001525/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_652ba6a6bd71084bc46c","text":"3. **Soft-life gratitude that remains accountable to the poor.** Evidence: ن ع م B002 (softness/easeful living), ع ي ل B001/B002 in 93:8 (poverty/dependents), ي ت م B001 in 93:9 (orphan), س ء ل B001 in 93:10 (requester). Structural cue: blessing is mentioned only after prohibitions regarding the orphan and asker. Mechanism: the packet supplies soft/easy life and vulnerable figures; I supply the accountability arrow that ease must be narrated in a way that sustains care for those lacking ease. Abductive move: a blessing misnarrated without the orphan/requester would become hardness. Reading change: \"speak of blessing\" becomes ethically conditioned gratitude.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000299/B006","root_000532/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_6c3957189c1dc522dd4d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:11:3\",\"root_000532\",[\"B001\"]],[\"93:11:4\",\"root_000299\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Açıklamanın konusu Rabbin nimeti olduğu için, söz kişinin kendisini öne çıkarmasından çok nimetin kaynağını görünür kılar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000299/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:6","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6ccff37e546c90fc5d4d","text":"6. **Becoming a tale without losing freshness.** Evidence: ح د ث B002/B004 (recent freshness; becoming a tale people tell), س م و B008 in 93:0 (good repute spread), ء خ ر B001/B002 in 93:4 (laterness/deferral). Structural cue: the final command comes after a sequence of past rescues and future promise. Mechanism: the packet supplies old events becoming told tales and recentness; I supply the paradox that retelling preserves freshness. Abductive move: a later narration can keep the first rescue newly active. Reading change: the ayah turns biography into exemplary living memory rather than static reminiscence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000299/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_73fcfd099dc8dd3513b3","text":"5. **Polishing the heart through narration.** Evidence: ح د ث B007 (polishing a blade or heart by admonition), ر ض و B001 in 93:5 (acceptance), ق ل ي B003 in 93:3 (aversion casting from heart), ر ح م B001 in 93:0 (tenderness). Structural cue: the surah moves from denied heart-aversion to satisfied acceptance and ends with speech. Mechanism: the packet supplies heart-polishing and aversion/acceptance branches; I supply the interior mechanism that speaking favor cleans away the rust of perceived abandonment. Abductive move: narration is therapeutic remembrance. Reading change: \"فحدث\" is not only outward report; it also keeps the heart from reverting to the suspicion of being hated or left.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001525/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_747ba2e2c1a794f3189a","text":"1. **Favor must become renewed speech.** Evidence: ن ع م B001 (bestowed favor/good condition), ر ب ب B002 (nurture/completion), ح د ث B003 (speech/report/conversation), ح د ث B001 (coming into being after not being). Structural cue: the surah ends by turning received favor into commanded utterance. Mechanism: the packet supplies blessing and speech-event branches; I supply the completion arrow that favor becomes socially real as a fresh report. Abductive move: narration is not ornamental; it causes the remembered favor to occur again in communal hearing. Reading change: \"speak\" means renew the favor as an event, not merely mention it.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8824532fa830baad9427","text":"No later ayah remains after 93:11. In the end-pass, its surprise was retroactive rather than forward-looking: it gathered almost every earlier mechanism—name, mercy, no-abandonment, delayed betterment, giving, sheltering, guiding, enriching, and restraint toward vulnerable people—into a single act of narrated favor. The final command therefore reads as the surah's disclosure device.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000299/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_974ec40a2188955ee2f8","text":"4. **The report can polish through admonition.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ح د ث:B007` includes polishing the heart through admonition. **Structural cue:** the command follows the paired imperatives concerning orphan and asker, so the report is embedded in an ethical reminder rather than isolated celebration. **Assigned role:** the polishing branch makes repeated narration a means of removing forgetfulness or hardness. **Abductive move:** I infer recollection of received care → softened remembrance → corrected conduct; the packet supplies command order but not this psychology explicitly. **Reading change:** `فَحَدِّثْ` can function as self- and community-admonition that keeps the care-to-duty relation bright.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000299/B001","root_000299/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_998e5024f22ea903cb17","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:11:4\",\"root_000299\",[\"B001\",\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Anlatma, gizli kalmış bakımı açığa çıkarıp geçmişte olanı ortak hafızada yeniden etkin kılar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001525/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_9bc35b68dfa59e4c4841","text":"3. **Favor is carried further as softened conduct.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ن ع م:B002` is softness and easeful living, and fixed `ن ع م:B010` is adding or carrying an action further; 93:9–10 forbid humiliating force and harsh speech. **Structural cue:** the two social prohibitions intervene between the remembered favors and the command to tell them. **Assigned role:** softness supplies favor's behavioral texture; “going further” supplies transmission from recipient to others. **Abductive move:** I infer received favor → softened power and speech → favor extended socially; every arrow is interpretive, though the ordered elements are in the packet. **Reading change:** speaking of the favor is credible when the favor has also altered how the addressee treats vulnerable people.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000299/B003","root_000532/B002","root_000532/B016","root_001525/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ad0f117bc7073056df26","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:11:2\",\"root_001525\",[\"B001\"]],[\"93:11:3\",\"root_000532\",[\"B002\",\"B016\"]],[\"93:11:4\",\"root_000299\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Anlatılması istenen nimet, soyut bir mülk değil, barındırma, yol gösterme ve yeterli kılma boyunca Rabbin aşama aşama yürüttüğü bakım sürecidir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B016","root_001525/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c543845c3fb5b62105bb","text":"1. **The commanded report names a process of nurture, not an abstract possession.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ن ع م:B001` is bestowed favor and well-being, fixed `ح د ث:B003` is speech or report, and fixed `ر ب ب:B002` is nurture and completion stage by stage; the benefaction scope within fixed `ر ب ب:B016` is also activated by adjacent `نِعْمَةِ`. **Structural cue:** 93:6–8 supply three concrete condition-to-remedy episodes, and `رَبِّكَ` recalls the agent of that sequence. **Assigned role:** favor names the whole beneficial process; Rabb supplies its continuing administration; report renders the process communicable. **Abductive move:** I infer that the nearby episodes are reportable instances of the favor, because the packet places them immediately before the command without explicitly equating them. **Reading change:** `فَحَدِّثْ` calls for diagnostic narration of how care operated across time, not a bare inventory of goods.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001525/B002","root_001525/B010"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ce0d9bdafb1d4caaf5d1","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:11:2\",\"root_001525\",[\"B002\",\"B010\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Nimetin yumuşaklık ve işi daha ileri taşıma boyutları, alınan iyiliği yetime ve isteyene karşı yumuşamış güç ve söz olarak başkalarına ulaştırır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001525/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d968e832b76255893332","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:11:2\",\"root_001525\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Otlayan hayvan çağrışımı, nimeti sabah, verme ve sunuyla bağlantılı maddi bir rızık ve paylaşım düzeni olarak okumayı önerir; bu hayvansal bağ açık değildir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000299/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ef34e292ff3c42512dc7","text":"2. **Disclosure that raises the name, not self-display.** Evidence: ح د ث B006 (disclosure/bringing out), س م و B005/B008 in 93:0 (name as raised designation; good repute spread), ء ل ه B002 in 93:0 (fixed divine name in invocation), ر ب ب B001 (Lordship/ownership). Structural cue: the final phrase \"favor of your Lord\" sends disclosure back to the opening divine name. Mechanism: the packet supplies disclosure plus raised-name branches; I supply the rule that the disclosed object is the Lord's favor, not autonomous self-boasting. Abductive move: right narration transfers publicity upward to the named giver.
